Both branches were live before the data was opened and this one fires by a very wide margin. **AGAINST THE CITED COMPARATORS, all on the same frozen 60 SLURP devel samples and the same frozen core**: A1'r closed-set decode routing **0.7833** (47/60, cited consumed 2026-08-18); P-FEW FS-NONE closed-set decode routing **0.8167** (49/60, in-session, consumed the same day); A0 free-form decode **~0.10** (cited, about 6/360 exact scenario hits); uniform chance over 18 labels **0.0556**; best single-label constant predictor (always `calendar`, the modal gold class) **0.1333**. **At 0.0500 the embedding router is below ALL of them, chance included, and roughly sixteen-fold below the closed-set decode router measured on the identical samples this same week.** **CONFUSION STRUCTURE -- A COLLAPSE, NOT MISROUTING**: only **5 of the 18 labels were ever predicted**, and two attractors absorb **55 of 60 samples (91.7 percent)** -- `general` 29, `play` 26, `transport` 3, `datetime` 1, `iot` 1, and **zero** for the other thirteen canonical labels INCLUDING `calendar`, the modal gold class with 8 of the 60 samples. The table therefore reads column-wise: `calendar->play` 6, `qa->general` 4, `play->general` 4, `iot->general` 3, `transport->play` 3, `cooking->play` 2, `music->play` 2, `news->general` 2, `weather->general` 2. **THE THREE CORRECT PREDICTIONS ARE COINCIDENCES OF THE COLLAPSE** (`004134` play/play cosine 0.0808, `005684` play/play 0.0236, `006603` general/general 0.0082 -- all three inside the two attractors). Descriptive, UNREGISTERED, no clause attached: re-pairing the exact same 60 predicted labels to the exact same 60 samples at random expects **4.30** hits, MORE than the 3 observed, and a permutation test shuffling only the prediction vector (N=100,000, seed 20260807) gives **P(hits >= 3) = 0.8774** with the shuffled distribution centred at 5. The observed agreement with gold is therefore fully accounted for by the prediction marginal alone: **there is NO detectable audio-content signal in the argmax**, and 0.0500 should be read as NO ROUTING rather than as weak routing. **THE GEOMETRY AGREES**: vectors are L2-normalized on both sides (`--embd-normalize` at its default 2, the registration's "cosine = dot"), and over the 60 winning (audio, label) pairs the top-1 cosine has max **0.0808**, mean **0.0377**, median **0.0366**, min **-0.0012** (one sample whose BEST label out of eighteen has a negative cosine), and **42 of 60 below 0.05** -- the nearest label is essentially orthogonal to the audio in every single case, so the argmax is picking the least-orthogonal of eighteen near-orthogonal directions, a text-side norm/direction artifact rather than a content match. The two-attractor collapse and the near-orthogonality are the same fact seen twice. **WHAT THIS DOES NOT LICENSE**: this is a negative on ONE CONSTRUCTION, not on embedding routing as a family, and three confounds stay unresolved -- (1) the core has NO contrastive objective (Qwen3-Omni is a generative decoder; nothing makes mean-pooled audio and text states co-locate, so the negative is best stated as a property of the frozen core's representation), (2) severe length/form asymmetry (a one-or-two-token bare label string against a multi-second clip at roughly 40 pooled positions, mean 3.04 s), and (3) the pooled vector is KNOWN to be dominated by non-content structure -- E-0's own binding measurement that a prompt prefix shifts the pooled vector MORE than swapping the audio does. That E-0 dilution finding and this collapse are one phenomenon: if format moves the representation more than content does, a content-based argmax over it must collapse onto whichever candidates sit nearest the format-determined centroid, and it did, onto two of them. A rescue attempt (label sentences or templated descriptions instead of bare strings, last-token or attention-weighted pooling instead of mean, or a learned projection) is a FRESH REGISTRATION and may NOT be run as a re-cut of these spent vectors. COST NOTE for the successor: the negative cost 78 contacts, 0.006 GPU-h (3.1 percent of ceiling), 7.55 s wall -- the embedding tier is cheap to test and the economics that motivated the probe survive; only this realization is dead. **ROUTING-TIER READING** (scheme scoreboard `docs/readiness/2026-08-19-scheme-scoreboard.md`, best-scheme statement v1): the tier offered two candidate mechanisms for high-order semantics, closed-set decode prompting (established) and whole-utterance representation matching (this probe); Run A closes the second on the SLURP carrier. Combined with the same-day P-FEW read, **two of the three candidate routing levers are now eliminated and both eliminations are clean rather than marginal** -- few-shot demonstration supply on the decode router gives no gain (BAL-NONE +0.0000) and bare mean-pooled embedding matching gives no routing at all (0.0500, below chance, permutation P=0.8774). The decode path is not merely the BEST router on this core; as of this read it is the ONLY one that works at all. The tier's remaining open direction is not a different REPRESENTATION but a different TARGET: P-FEW localized more than half the decode router's residual error into one confusable neighbourhood (`play` collapsing into `music`/`audio`/`news`), and targeted corrective supply aimed at a measured confusion is the one routing-tier idea this arc has not tested. **EFFECT ON THE P-TARGET DESIGN GATE**: Run A removes one candidate source of the router-confidence signal -- **cosine margin from this embedding space is NOT a usable confidence signal**, because a margin computed over near-orthogonal collapsed directions cannot rank samples by routing difficulty. If P-TARGET is registered, its confidence signal must come from the decode path (label-token logits, closed-set agreement across resamples or prompt variants), not from this representation. Run B's read is the remaining input to that gate. **SCOPE**: descriptive, discovery-tier, n=60 SLURP devel, 18 labels, ONE run, same-distribution, one carrier, one core, ONE pooling mode (mean), ONE text rendering (bare canonical label string), no instruct prefix. The permutation check is descriptive and unregistered. NC CARRIED: SLURP audio CC BY-NC 4.0 on every audio-derived number. Gold scenarios were used scoring-side only and never entered a runtime path. The top-5 figure is worse than chance outright: **0.0273 against a uniform top-5 rate of 5/152 = 0.0329** -- the router's five-best list is LESS likely to contain the gold tool than five rows drawn at random. Both recoveries land on collapse-attractor tools (`tier2_parametric:004217` -> `streamCamera`, cosine -0.1047; `tier7_multiturn:000318` -> `setHumidifierState`, cosine -0.0707), not on tools reached by discrimination. **The 183 are NOT opened and stay exactly as V4 left them**, now with one measured failed rescue attempt rather than an open question. **NON-DEGRADATION ON THE 217 (reported, NO CLAUSE attaches to any number in this sentence group)**: the embedding router retains **1/217 = 0.0046** of the selections the decode router already had right -- BELOW the uniform chance rate of 1/152 = 0.0066, with the permutation baseline expecting 1.24 hits and giving P(hits >= 1) = 0.7358 -- against the decode router's 217/217 = 1.0000 on that population by construction; whole-population, decode **217/400 = 0.5425** against embedding **3/400 = 0.0075**. Composition arithmetic, recorded because it is the only form such a router could be deployed in: as a REPLACEMENT it loses **214 of 400**; as a rescue/argmax-fallback applied only where decode fails it adds **2**; as a confidence-gated fallback it would need a gate that this space does not provide. No composition is net-positive on this carrier. **CONFUSION STRUCTURE -- THE SAME COLLAPSE AS RUN A, ON A 152-WAY PROBLEM**: only **15 of 152** tools were ever predicted, `stopCharging` alone taking **151/400 = 37.8 percent**, the top two **213/400 = 53.3 percent** and the top five **343/400 = 85.8 percent**; **137 registry rows were never the nearest neighbour of any clip**. On the 183 there are 11 distinct predictions (`stopCharging` 62, `setHumidifierState` 33, `streamCamera` 25, `getNetworkHealth` 24, `setHvacMode` 15) and on the 217 there are 13 (`stopCharging` 89, `getNetworkHealth` 33, `setHumidifierState` 29, `setHvacMode` 20, `setVoiceGuidance` 15). The table reads column-wise with no visible relation between gold tool and absorbing attractor -- `setColor->stopCharging` 4, `getAirQuality->stopCharging` 3, `getAirQuality_diag->streamCamera` 3, `setColor->setHumidifierState` 3, `setGroupLightState->streamCamera` 3, `setLighting->stopCharging` 3. **EVERY ONE OF THE 400 WINNING COSINES IS NEGATIVE**: max **-0.0494**, mean **-0.1253**, median **-0.1177**, min **-0.2541**, and **0/400** positive. With L2-normalized vectors on both sides, the BEST of 152 candidate rows is ANTI-ALIGNED with the audio on every single sample; Run A's near-orthogonality (mean +0.038) becomes uniform anti-alignment here, and the argmax is selecting the least-negative of 152 negative similarities -- a text-side geometry artifact, of which the 15-tool collapse is the same fact seen from the label side. **THIS CARRIER REMOVES RUN A's STRONGEST CONFOUND**: Run A's text side was a bare one-or-two-token canonical label and "severe length/form asymmetry against a multi-second clip" was a live explanation; Run B's text side is the FULL SHIPPED REGISTRY ROW (`data.audio2tool.render_registry_row`, `strip_defaults=False` -- name, description, parameter schema), an order of magnitude richer and longer, against clips averaging 5.4 s -- and the result is WORSE, not better (accuracy at chance, top-5 below chance, every cosine negative). Making the text side richer does not help, so length/form asymmetry is NOT the explanation and the surviving one is the one Run A named but could not isolate: **the frozen core has no contrastive objective, so its mean-pooled audio states and mean-pooled text states do not occupy a shared space.** Two carriers, two candidate-set sizes (18 vs 152), two text renderings, one conclusion. **P-TARGET DESIGN-GATE READING** (scoreboard design-queue item 3, registered as needing the P-EMB reads; both are now in): the gate resolves NEGATIVELY for the embedding channel and leaves the problem's importance unchanged. (1) **The embedding channel cannot supply the confidence signal** -- P-TARGET's implicit candidate was cosine margin, and a margin computed over a collapsed, anti-aligned similarity field ranks the attractor geometry rather than the samples' routing difficulty; measured on two carriers, not a marginal call. (2) **The targeting problem is unchanged and still binding** -- V4 measured the gap between targeted supply (+8 to +10 EM conversions on the gold-identified 120-id headroom set) and blind supply (-26 to -28 EM samples over the 217 ids where the rule is defined) at **34 to 38 EM samples wide**, an ORG/SUPPLY-side selection problem and not a supply-form problem; P-EMB-2 was the one registered instrument that could have changed the shape of that account by opening the excluded 183, and it did not, so the 45.75-percent-undefined denominator disclosure stays attached and the gap gains no new instrument from this arc. (3) **Where the signal must come from instead** -- the decode path, for which this arc has already produced measured gold-free candidates: label-token logprobs under the closed-set prompt (no extra call cost), and cross-prompt disagreement, whose statistics P-FEW happens to supply -- flagging the samples where FS-NONE and FS-BAL choose different labels marks **6 of 60**, on all 6 at least one arm is wrong, FS-NONE itself is wrong on **3 of the 6** for a flag precision of **0.50** against a base error rate of **11/60 = 0.1833**, a **2.73x lift** at recall **3/11 = 0.27**; three-way disagreement across FS-NONE/FS-BAL/FS-SKEW marks 9 at precision 0.33 (1.82x lift, same recall), so TWO arms are the better flag than three. Those figures are descriptive, unregistered, n=6 and carry no clause; they are recorded because they are the only measured gold-free targeting signal this program currently has. **RECOMMENDATION INPUT for a P-TARGET registration (an input, not a design, and not an authorization)**: register the confidence signal as a DECODE-PATH disagreement or logprob statistic; pre-register its precision/recall against the MEASURED base error rate rather than an assumed one; and scope the target population to the ids where an EM-signed rule is defined, EXPLICITLY EXCLUDING the 183, which this read has now shown are not reachable by the embedding route either. Any design assuming an embedding-derived confidence score is refuted before it starts. **SYNTHESIS -- ROUTING-TIER SCORECARD** (full version in the verdict document's appendix): closed-set decode routing (CITED A1'r 0.7833 = 47/60 plus in-session FS-NONE 0.8167 = 49/60, a **0.78-0.82 range across two sessions**, `{exact: 60}` mapping and 60/60 non-fallback under three prompt constructions) remains ESTABLISHED and, after this arc, is still the ONLY routing lever that works at all; few-shot demonstration supply on that router gives **no gain** (FS-BAL 0.8167, delta +0.0000, CI [-0.0667, +0.0667]) with equivalence NOT certified, and its skew arm shows a small real ICL-noise cost (+0.0667, CI [+0.0167, +0.1167]) with **zero** label-prior capture (0/59 incremental, marginal unchanged 3->3->3); bare mean-pooled embedding matching on SLURP is **below chance** (0.0500 vs 0.0556, 5 of 18 labels ever predicted, permutation P=0.8774); registry-row mean-pooled embedding matching on Audio2Tool is **at chance on top-1 and below chance on top-5 and on the 217** (0.0109 / 0.0273 / 0.0046, 15 of 152 tools, 400/400 negative cosines). Two candidate improvements, both eliminated cleanly. Two limits discovered inside the arc travel with every tier number: cross-session reproduction on the 60-sample SLURP routing instrument is **+/-2 samples (+/-0.0333)**, larger than the primary few-shot contrast and two-thirds of the +/-0.05 equivalence band; and the residual routing error is **structurally concentrated, not diffuse** (6 of 11 SLURP decode errors are `play` collapsing into `music`/`audio`/`news`, while 9 of 18 classes are perfect on every arm), which is the mechanical reason few-shot never had the headroom its +0.10 floor demanded. The arc's founding premise -- that the word-level acoustic resonance law does not extend to the label level -- is now evidenced at **2/2** (P-TRAP 0/32 cross-sample content capture; P-FEW 0/59 incremental label-prior capture). The tier's remaining untested idea is **corrective, targeted supply aimed at the measured confusion neighbourhood**, the routing-tier twin of the filling tier's supply-targeting problem and the natural object of a P-CORR-style design. **SCOPE**: descriptive, discovery-tier, n=400 Audio2Tool discovery split, 152 registry rows, ONE run, same-distribution, one carrier for this row and two across the appendix, one frozen core, ONE pooling mode (mean), ONE text rendering (full shipped registry row), no instruct prefix. The permutation checks, the composition arithmetic and the disagreement-flag statistics are descriptive and unregistered and carry no clause. The 183/217 partition is a property of ONE consumed A0 attempt (V4 bound 3), not an established stable set. NC CARRIED: Audio2Tool CC BY-NC 4.0 on every audio-derived number here, and SLURP CC BY-NC 4.0 on the appendix's SLURP numbers. Gold tools and gold scenarios were used scoring-side only and never entered a runtime path. No test split was touched. No superiority, generalization, deployment, or paper-scale claim attaches. |

The equivalence branch was equally live (32 paired samples can in principle put the whole CI inside +/-0.03), so neither branch was suppressed and no affirmative outcome is being reported on an unreachable clause. **VERDICT: ACOUSTIC-MEDIATED** (the section-6 CI-gated evaluator, authoritative; the registered section-3 point thresholds are met as well). capture(V1) = **29/32 = 0.9062**; capture(V2) = **0/32 = 0.0000**; rate difference **+0.9062** (0.90625); raw difference **+29**; sigma_hat = **0.0519** (0.051882) from the paired cluster bootstrap over the 32 common-denominator samples, N=10,000 replicates, seed 20260807; 90 percent CI **[+0.8125, +0.9688]**. CLAUSE MARGINS: ACOUSTIC-MEDIATED point (rate diff >= +0.10 AND raw diff >= 6) MET, clearing by **+0.8062** and **+23**; ACOUSTIC-MEDIATED CI-gated (point met AND CI low > 0) MET, CI low +0.8125 clearing the 0 gate by **+0.8125** with the entire CI above +0.81; KEY-ONLY-MATCHING point (abs(rate diff) <= 0.03) NOT MET, over by **+0.8762**; KEY-ONLY-MATCHING CI-gated NOT MET -- it failed on the point estimate before the CI was consulted, and the CI is disjoint from the equivalence band by +0.7825 at its nearest edge; INCONCLUSIVE-TRAP not reached. Every registered floor is cleared by roughly an order of magnitude; this is not a threshold artifact. **THE CONTROL RETURNED AN EXACT ZERO**: not one of the 32 cross-percept rows emitted its appended trap, across 49 flown V2 entries from 31 distinct donor samples. The capture detector is the SAME code path on both arms (same normalization, same exact-match test, same per-row trap list), so 29-vs-0 cannot be a detector asymmetry, and it is direct evidence that the core does not emit roster items opportunistically -- supplied wrong values with no acoustic relation to this audio are ignored completely. **PER-SAMPLE TABLE** (32 usable, full table in the verdict document and `verdict.json`): 29 captured on V1, 0 on V2; the three non-capturing usable samples are `000943`, `005453`, `007852`, ALL at K=1, the minimum dose. Unregistered descriptive with no clause attached, recorded because it is monotone -- capture by trap count: K=1 **15/18** (0.833), K=2 **11/11** (1.000), K=3 **3/3** (1.000): every sample offered two or more of its own mishearings took at least one. **K=0 STARVATION CAVEAT** (carried on every citation): 28 of 60 samples flew at K=0 and are outside the denominator -- only **5** because A0 emitted no wrong value at all (`000444`, `005536`, `005743`, `012667`, `017009`), and **23** because the fail-closed gold zero-overlap check removed EVERY candidate. In total that check dropped **42 of 91** harvested V1 candidates across **34** samples (11 of which retained at least one and are in the usable 32), plus 8 on V2, leaving 49 flown entries per arm. The check is correct and was enforced fail-closed three times at binding, but it is a SELECTION OPERATOR with a direction: a surviving V1 trap is a value the core produced from this audio that matches NO gold slot of this sample under any type -- a genuine misperception -- while a wiped-out sample is one whose A0 errors were all gold-adjacent (type misassignment or slot routing rather than mishearing). The usable 32 are therefore ENRICHED for genuine misperception and the excluded 23 are exactly the stratum where a self-percept trap would be least distinguishable from correct supply, so **0.9062 is an upper-leaning estimate of a population rate and must never be narrated as 'the core re-commits to its own mishearing 90 percent of the time on SLURP devel'**. The VERDICT nonetheless survives the most adverse available reweighting: assume zero V1 capture on all 28 excluded samples and score the whole 60 -> capture(V1) 29/60 = **0.4833**, capture(V2) 0/60 = 0.0000, rate diff **+0.4833** (clearing the +0.10 floor by +0.3833) and raw diff **+29** (clearing 6 by +23). Excluded samples can only lower V1 capture, never raise V2's exact zero, so no reweighting can flip the sign or the verdict; what the exclusion compromises is the MAGNITUDE, not the existence or direction of acoustic mediation. Secondary consequence: the trap supply is far sparser than the 91-value harvest implies and K tops out at 3 against a cap of 5, so the dose-response above is measured over a compressed range. **GUARDRAILS** (registered, descriptive, no clause attaches; cited comparator = the consumed P-SLU A3 arm's official pooled SLU-F1 0.5961280580450439, pinned as `CONSUMED_A3_SLU_F1` and taken verbatim from `2026-08-18-pslu-read/verdict.json`): SLU-F1 V1 **0.5003** (delta **-0.0958** vs A3) against V2 **0.5907** (delta **-0.0054**) -- trapping costs task quality and ONLY self-percept trapping does, which controls the roster-size confound outright since V2 appended exactly as many entries from the same source distribution at matched lengths. The arm-level figure is DILUTED: SLU-F1 is pooled over all 60 rows per arm and 28 of them carry no trap (identical plain A3-form requests on both arms), so the whole -0.0958 is generated by the 32 trapped samples and the cost where a trap exists is materially larger -- a subset SLU-F1 over the usable 32 was NOT computed by the registered suite and is NOT claimed. Induced substitutions V1 **11** / V2 **3**, and they localize precisely: 8 of V1's 11 fall on usable samples and 3 on excluded K=0 samples, while ALL 3 of V2's fall on those SAME three excluded ids (`005743`, `014263`, `015375`) whose V1 and V2 requests are identical constructions at K=0 -- an incidental in-read determinism check. Netting the shared baseline out, **the trap channel induced 8 substitutions on V1 and 0 on V2 over the same 32 samples**. Capture is not damage and the gap is informative: only 8 of the 29 captures registered an induced substitution, so **21 of 29 emitted the trap without displacing an aligned gold filler** -- self-percept contamination is UBIQUITOUS (90.6 percent of trapped samples) but SELECTIVELY COSTLY (28 percent of captures displace correct content). **INTEGRITY**: parse rate 1.0000 and empty rate 0.0000 on BOTH arms (no degenerate rows anywhere); `recheck_mismatch_count` **0** -- every per-sample TP/FP/FN reproduced the committed frozen scorer exactly on recheck; `harness_defect_reasons` EMPTY; flight status COMPLETED with error None; 60/60 rows on each arm across 120 distinct (arm, sample_id) pairs. **WHY THIS IS NOT THE 'IT WOULD HAVE SAID THAT ANYWAY' ARTIFACT**: with no supply the core scores 0.1741 SLU-F1 on these same 60 samples (P-SLU A0, reproduced bit-identically by P-KEY's AUD-NOSUP arm across sessions -- CITED, not recomputed here) and with the plain legal roster it scores 0.5961 (A3), so the roster MOVES THE CORE OFF most of its A0 percept; V1 re-supplies that abandoned percept in the roster's own item shape and the core TAKES IT BACK on 29 of 32 samples while losing ~0.096 pooled SLU-F1. The core does not merely persist in an error it was going to make -- it REVERSES a correction it had already made, when the error is re-presented as legal evidence. V2 independently rules out the roster-mechanics explanation (identical appended position, count, rendering, length distribution and source distribution; the ONLY difference is whether the value came from this sample's audio or another's; it captured zero). **WHAT THIS ADDS TO P-KEY'S INCONCLUSIVE ON THE SPEECH TERM**: P-TRAP does NOT fire P-KEY's clauses and cannot -- P-KEY's I is a NET TASK-QUALITY BENEFIT interaction on a partial-credit metric while P-TRAP's capture difference is a MECHANISM-PRESENCE measurement on the matching step; nothing here re-opens or re-cuts P-KEY's registered thresholds. What it changes is which explanations of P-KEY's null remain available: the deflationary account that **the audio is ONLY a retrieval key**, with all roster matching done text-side against the core's own transcript, is now FALSIFIED as a general description of matching -- if matching were text-side, a wrong value with no acoustic relation to this audio would be no less matchable than one with, and V2 captured 0/32 against V1's 29/32. The joint statement is therefore narrower than either probe alone: (i) the speech channel PARTICIPATES in supply USE (CI-gated, margin about 8x the floor) -- not a key, a live discriminator at match time; (ii) its measured NET contribution to task quality remains unresolved (P-KEY I = +0.0463, CI crossing zero, dead-zone point estimate) -- participation is not benefit; (iii) the one place that participation has been MEASURED rather than inferred, it is a COST (-0.0958 pooled SLU-F1, 8 induced substitutions vs V2's 0). The tension between a large mediation effect (+0.906) and a small uncertain benefit (+0.046) reconciles, as a READING and not a measurement, as: the acoustic percept is consulted at match time, but on this carrier the core's own transcript already carries nearly all the information that consultation uses (P-KEY: a 53.6-percent-macro-WER self-transcript costs -0.0001 at baseline and +0.046 under supply). **QUALIFICATION SECTION 2b stays OPEN and its freeze stays HELD** -- P-TRAP was never a section-2b decision instrument and does not become one. What 2b may now record as settled is a new line: the speech-term question has an AFFIRMATIVE MECHANISM-SIDE answer (matching is acoustically mediated, CI-gated, on the SLURP carrier) beside an UNRESOLVED BENEFIT-SIDE answer; the honest framing is no longer 'speech knowledge participates, or the audio is only a key' because the second disjunct is dead, but 'speech participation in USE is established; its net value in the supply loop is not'. **NEW USE-SIDE RISK, citable at discovery tier** beside C2's harm channel and C5's deployment reading: SELF-PERCEPT CONTAMINATION -- if a control plane's evidence roster can contain the core's own earlier outputs on the same audio (which any pipeline with a recall/caching/self-supply stage can produce by accident), the core re-commits at 0.9062 on the trapped population, reversing repairs the legal roster had already made, and displaces gold content on 28 percent of captures; the implied mitigation is an ORG-side provenance rule (never re-supply the core's own prior emissions for the same episode) and the V2 construction is the control that makes it checkable. **SCOPE**: descriptive, discovery-tier, n=60 SLURP devel of which 32 usable, single run, same-distribution, one carrier, an enriched trap stratum per the K=0 caveat; sigma_hat is measured on exactly one flight. 'Acoustically mediated' means matching is sensitive to the audio; it does NOT identify what about the audio (phonetic similarity, prosodic alignment, or coarse this-audio-vs-that-audio discrimination) and the design cannot distinguish those. NC CARRIED: SLURP audio CC BY-NC 4.0 on every audio-derived number. No test split was touched. No superiority, generalization, deployment, or paper-scale claim attaches. |

**The single scientific X108 read finds bounded AgentLoop headroom but no controller ready for GATE-C36.** The hash-verified np16 read (`348102a95aae8fa10f571959f4053e15ee75776c8a8e84c319858d6c61b4a1e0`) scores the embedded K1-M1 primary at 89/108 and the registered unanimous AgentLoop at 90/108: two wrong-to-correct changes and one correct-to-wrong change, for a net gain of one. The gain appears in only one of three speaker-grouped folds; the other two folds are unchanged. The preregistered speaker-grouped out-of-fold contextual policy scores 87/108, with zero wrong-to-correct and two correct-to-wrong changes, so it is rejected. Nevertheless, the complete action table is non-degenerate: 31 queries are action-sensitive, seven observable states have reward spread, seven primary errors are recoverable by at least one recorded action, and the diagnostic action oracle is 96/108, seven above M1. **A model-free server-log analysis identifies prompt-cache contamination as the new causal diagnosis.** The wide-batch service log contains exactly 200 prompt-evaluation timing records: the first 100 are R6 and the next 100 are R8. R6 evaluated 143,253 prompt tokens in total, median 1,824 per request, with zero one-token cache reuses. R8 evaluated only 67,929 prompt tokens, median 461.5, with 42/100 requests evaluating one token because the repeated prompt was already cached. This explains why declared-token throughput doubled while measured GPU utilization fell. R8 remains rejected under its prospective gates, but it does not causally reject wide kernels on fresh requests. Any later serving diagnosis must use previously uncached speech/query combinations or disable server prompt caching, persist actual prompt-evaluation tokens and cache-hit counts, and compute throughput from work actually evaluated rather than declared input tokens. This is a new diagnosis, not authorization for an undifferentiated batch sweep.

**Dataset analysis fixes the information requirements and the next cold engineering cell before model contact.** SLURP train contains 11,514 sentences, 50,628 real recordings, 18 scenarios, 91 fine-grained intents, 46 action values, 60 observed scenario--action pairs, and 55 entity types. At recording weight the scenario prior entropy is 3.944 bits; conditioning on action leaves 1.091 bits, and conditioning jointly on action plus the observed entity-type signature still leaves 0.611 bits. Therefore operation cues alone are information-incomplete: speech-conditioned target/object/context evidence remains necessary. The frozen knowledge artifacts cover all 18 scenario definitions and all 60 scenario--action pairs through 18 target plus 60 operation factors; every card has five train-only positive examples and a contrastive boundary. However, only 30 explicit graph boundaries cover 66 cross-scenario pairs sharing an action, leaving a legitimate knowledge/AgentLoop optimization surface rather than evidence that speech failed. **The R10-R2 structural audit identifies one bounded mechanism defect and does not authorize an effectiveness read.** Audit SHA-256 is `4f96fcccadf0ff7631e3b2662cca23e73fd249104402aae5553750cfaafb2e6b`. It proves 144 decisions, 134 three-contact and ten five-contact trajectories, 143 valid factor states, ten registered boundary branches, 14 action-variable trajectories, eight key-order-variable queries, exact original-speech carriage on 452/452 contacts, closed durable accounting, and zero mmproj failure. The sole mechanism failure is task `canonical:slurp:train:013414:audio-1490019231`: primary and retrieval both selected `K013`, with retrieved candidates `K013,K007,K001`, but the candidate-action call produced no parser-admissible key/factor binding and terminated after three contacts at `backtrack:invalid-candidate-action-state`. No response text, label, prediction meaning, or correctness was opened. This is a structured-state reliability defect, not evidence that the speech query or knowledge store lacks the answer.

**Logprob remains outside the registered action space.** The pinned llama.cpp build exposes generated-token logprobs, but no receipt-bound interface currently provides the required speech-query-to-key conditional compatibility score. Historical target-entity logprob extraction also yielded zero usable targets in 17 cases. Reopen this decision only if the active inference boundary can return an auditable query-conditioned key score or dataset analysis identifies a specific missing information quantity that such a score would supply.

**The next M11 prompt optimization is implemented model-free as a versioned policy, without adding a script or model contact.** Policy `speech-conditioned-adaptive-evidence-gap-v2` reuses the existing matcher and contact dispatcher while moving deterministic graph knowledge into the llama.cpp JSON grammar: one `oneOf` branch per candidate binds `key_id` to only that key's registered factors, and `prefixItems` requires the first factor to be an operation. The local pinned llama.cpp source at commit `04ccc72a2` implements both constructs. The v1 policy remains readable and reproducible; the existing experiment config now points prospectively to v2. This removes the observed cross-key/operation structural degree of freedom without adding an information-free retry. It does not by itself authorize another inference flight or relax the failed GPU gate.

**The logprob exclusion was revalidated against upstream documentation and the pinned local inference source with zero model contact.** The official llama.cpp server API and local commit `04ccc72a2207` expose probabilities only for generated completion positions. The reranking handler requires a string query, string documents, rank pooling, and a reranker-compatible model; it cannot accept the registered speech-feature packet and is not available from the current always-on Omni profile. Generated-token logprob and reranking therefore remain outside the M11 state and training-free action space. Reconsideration requires a receipt-pinnable speech-query-to-fixed-key conditional sequence score on the same decoder-only service, with no second model or profile replacement. Manager PID 612 / server PID 620 were not contacted, stopped, or modified.

**A model-free SLURP information audit replaces the arbitrary top-30 graph prospectively without changing historical artifacts.** The governed train set contains 11,514 utterances and 50,628 natural recordings. Recording-weighted scenario entropy is 3.944140 bits, action reduces it to 1.090694 bits, and action plus entity-type signature reduces it to 0.611382 bits. The historical graph selects 30 of all 153 scenario pairs and covers only 12 of the 66 pairs with nominal shared actions. After excluding every registered query utterance and normalized sentence, an action-support floor of five removes 11 low-support cooking-query pairs and leaves 55 stable shared-action ambiguities. Boundary policy `shared-action-entity-boundaries-v2` admits at most three entity types per side with count at least two, prevalence at least one percent, and Laplace-smoothed conditional-rate ratio at least five. All 55 stable pairs have bilateral evidence under this frozen rule.

**The v3 decision graph and existing M11 configuration are implemented model-free and do not authorize inference.** The existing `extend-speech-native-binding` CLI generated `configs/knowledge/slurp-shared-action-entity-decision-graph-v3.json` with 78 factors, 55 complete stable boundaries, and SHA-256 `07e51acc91b91636ae0451f5b9b2ebe719464140427b024701028621ccdeb6fb`. Reproduction of the historical v2 policy remained byte-identical at SHA-256 `c8bf25bacf0344a1c47fdb8659900636d8f669dec768d3580aae409de9533abb`. The existing M11 engineering config now points to v3; no experiment script was added. Focused regression is 176 passed and targeted lint passes. The resident decoder remains healthy and untouched. A fresh label-blind structural/GPU gate still needs its own registered exposure row, and the failed 95/90 utilization requirement remains in force.

**A model-free GPU-gate audit replaces the client-latency occupancy proxy prospectively.** R9/R10 selected saturated samples by reconstructing live HTTP intervals, so their occupancy figures include client response parsing and trace work that can outlive llama-server processing. Their wall time, token work, and throughput remain valid; their occupancy values are retained only as historical concurrency proxies and cannot support a causal scheduler diagnosis. The existing resource sampler now binds each GPU observation to a strict `/slots` snapshot from the same receipt-bound resident service and records busy/total slots plus sampling skew. New admission requires exact slot source, 100-percent slot-sample coverage, zero probe failures, at most 500 ms skew, the registered slot count, at least 90-percent mean true occupancy, and the unchanged saturated-sample and 95/90 GPU thresholds. The active PID 620 endpoint was read once without a model request and returned 16 unique slots, all with boolean `is_processing`, zero busy while idle. No model, prompt, packet, response, cache, or parameter was contacted or changed, and the service was not restarted.

**The paired short probes establish cache-effective speech-first AgentLoop execution but do not pass the GPU-tail admission.** Both finalized 16 label-blind trajectories and 50 contacts with the exact speech packet on 50/50 requests, zero parse/request/completion/OOM/spill/runaway/mmproj failure, zero RAM prompt-state event, 21,312 MiB peak VRAM, and no model reload. Speech-query-prefix-v2 evaluates exactly 16 speech chunks in two feature batches (one batch spans 15 slots), evaluates 47,098 of 80,110 declared prompt tokens, and finishes in 108.036 seconds at 448.214 actual-work tokens/s; GPU median/P10 are 99/86 and saturated slot occupancy is 94.242 percent. Dual-prefix-v2 also evaluates exactly 16 chunks but fragments them into 15 feature batches. Although it declares 33,132 additional prompt tokens, only 359 additional tokens are evaluated and wall time rises only 0.932 seconds, proving that its stable key index is amortized inside each trajectory. Its GPU P10 falls to 79 and saturated occupancy to 91.755 percent. Thus the primary layout is stable policy → speech → stable text knowledge → dynamic Agent state: it preserves both speech and knowledge KV without putting text prefill in front of speech batching. The generic receipt's `prompt_evaluation_ratio_at_least_90=false` is a cache-probe gate mismatch, because avoiding evaluation is the intended mechanism; historical receipts remain unchanged. Neither cell passes the frozen GPU P10 90 threshold or the known 500-ms clock-binding threshold, so no larger admission or semantic read is authorized. Paired mechanical artifact: `docs/checks/speech-aware-evidence-acquisition/2026-08-23-m11-prefix-probe-dual-live/paired-mechanical-result.json`. Flight SHA-256 values are `9871d2decce391492c3cfda40115b1073915e7c6c1736a74fab379e9945d3d11` / `246268cfb135645ac45e7dfa2011a0ff34dad103e08dda67cd22c2495dc0447f`; trace SHA-256 values are `56a5077d80674bae26335270089ca70bdc1db83f0dbd33dc45006f169ad31ba2` / `9ab6cc7df097d1c930dc43386742d4aab68b3aa5952b0a67b1a6f2e4984db4ae`. Prediction meaning, correctness, labels, transcripts, rewards, and generated-token probabilities remain unread. PID 607 remains healthy and resident.

The same driver binds both prefix orders to `active-hbm-only-v1`: GPU KV offload stays enabled, `--cache-prompt --cache-ram 0 --no-cache-idle-slots` is mandatory, host prompt-state serialization is false, and per-slot HBM-to-CPU migration is explicitly reported unsupported. Adding global `--no-kv-offload`, nonzero RAM cache, or idle-slot serialization now fails before model contact. WSL-only verification is 420 passed / 1 skipped with targeted lint clean; replaying the two immutable log windows yields 16 chunks / 2 batches / 8.0 mean slots for speech-query and 16 / 15 / 1.0667 for dual.
